Like little, you know, anch- not anchovies, like herring type that have been fried in oil. Says, "If you are hungry, please eat. Welcome to Pyhlla."

%% u3148:0-42, u3149:0-18, u3150:0-39, u3151:0-18, u3152:0-43 %%
DM: Skalme told me little about what you know. Just that you were seeking sacred waters and perhaps could solve our problem. So I will start the story at the beginning.

%% u3153:0-3 %%
Unknown: Hm.

%% u3154:0-34, u3155:0-26, u3156:0-19, u3157:0-27, u3158:0-15, u3159:0-10, u3160:0-39, u3161:0-7 %%
DM: Some years ago, a man named Urgall was exiled from the Skaer. He- He was not, uh, he was not part of, uh, my, uh, Askunya, my community, and I do not know the full story of his crimes.

%% u3162:0-11, u3163:0-8, u3164:0-81, u3165:0-31, u3166:0-64, u3167:0-138, u3168:0-18, u3169:0-25 %%
DM: But he left to seek, well, I guess wealth, but not to enrich the community, enrich, to enrich himself, in opposition to his community. For the past four years, he has gained a reputation as a pirate, mostly preying on Tollander merchants passing south of here on the way to the distant trade routes to Ursk and faraway places to the east. It is said that he made a deal with a demon.

%% u3170:0-4 %%
Unknown: Hmm.

%% u3171:0-30, u3172:0-4, u3173:0-5, u3174:0-28, u3175:0-30, u3176:0-3, u3177:0-43, u3178:0-58, u3179:0-19, u3180:0-18, u3181:0-61, u3182:0-18, u3183:0-21, u3184:0-3, u3185:0-32, u3186:0-49, u3187:0-11, u3188:0-36, u3189:0-29 %%
DM: Acquired some sorcerous magic. Hmm. About 10 months ago in the spring, for reasons unbeknownst to us, his attention turned to the treasures of Vetta. The sacred island of Vetta has had a temple of Kaikkea for thousands of years. It is also a place where those not blessed by the goddess are forbidden to step, and those who come in deception or evil, who try to take the wealth of Vetta, who try to leave in opposition to Kaikkea's will, are cursed. Their ships cannot sail out of port. The water turns against them.

%% u3190:0-4 %%
Unknown: Hmm.

%% u3191:0-67, u3192:0-22, u3193:0-76, u3194:0-33, u3195:0-15, u3196:0-19, u3197:0-23, u3198:0-59, u3199:0-47, u3200:0-34, u3201:0-6, u3202:0-11, u3203:0-34 %%
DM: So few in our legends have ever attempted to steal from this place. But Urgall landed, and only one of the small community of priests and lay servants that lived there managed to flee and return to us. And he spoke of slaughter and fire. Several of our warriors sailed to try and deal with this threat and never returned. It's now been seven months since Urgall landed, and nine of our best soldiers have sought to land and purify the island without success.

%% u3204:0-29, u3205:0-20, u3206:0-42, u3207:0-25, u3208:0-25, u3209:0-23, u3210:0-17 %%
DM: [coughs] So a few months ago, our district decided we had no choice but to ask for outsiders, and messages were sent to Kaupa and Iskra in Tollen to recruit adventurers. And a group came.

%% u3211:0-4 %%
Unknown: Hmm.

%% u3212:0-22, u3213:0-21, u3214:0-56 %%
DM: Tollender mercenaries. Kaikkea blessed them, welcomed them on their journey, and they never returned.

%% u3217:0-11 %%

%% u3352:0-18 %%

%% u3353:0-5 %%

%% u3357:0-15, u3358:0-30 %%

%% u3359:0-42, u3360:0-29 %%

%% u3361:0-5 %%

%% u3362:0-39 %%

%% u3372:0-3 %%

%% u3373:0-18, u3374:0-19, u3375:0-61, u3376:0-17, u3377:0-15 %%

%% u3379:0-3, u3380:0-31 %%

%% u3381:0-5 %%

Wellby: Yeah. Uh, as we pull around the fire, I think I'll, uh, ask, "Seeker, I've heard stories that I never knew if they were true or not, that in the deeps of the earth there are fiery places, that there are demons in the deeps. Is that true?"

%% u3437:0-33, u3438:0-29, u3439:0-29 %%
Seeker: Demons in the deeps of the earth? Uh, you'd think I would know. Tim, what do I know? [laughs]

%% u3440:0-3 %%

%% u3441:0-11 %%
Wellby: Make it up.

%% u3442:0-29, u3443:0-30, u3444:0-31, u3445:0-25 %%

%% u3446:0-62, u3447:0-25, u3448:0-30 %%
Seeker: One thing that the dwarves have learned as you descend through the crust, as we call it, is that it warms up as you go.

%% u3449:0-3 %%
Wellby: Oh.

%% u3450:0-20, u3451:0-29, u3452:0-45, u3453:0-29, u3454:0-29 %%
Seeker: The minerals become, uh, more uniform and s- hard. Uh, but there are also, uh, s- uh, strange... Oh, what's the word for that? Bubbles and other exclusions-

%% u3455:0-2 %%
Wellby: Mm

%% u3456:0-60, u3457:0-91, u3458:0-18 %%
Seeker: ... uh, from the stone that you suddenly penetrate into, and those are interesting to explore, but we don't always hear back from the people that try to go deep into them.

%% u3459:0-5 %%
Unknown: Uh...

%% u3460:0-36, u3461:0-67 %%
Wellby: What's the deepest you've ever gone? Like, have you been as far below the earth as we've flown above it?

%% u3462:0-28, u3463:0-178, u3464:0-34, u3465:0-31, u3466:0-70, u3467:0-27, u3468:0-67, u3469:0-20 %%
Seeker: There's one that's used, uh, not exactly as a tourist attraction, 'cause people don't, you know, travel high up into the mountains at great cost and discomfort to visit this thing, but for curiosity seekers, uh, there is the occasional visit. It's a, um... If you imagine a, a spherical space large enough to easily park our ship, but it is, uh, f- full halfway to the top, uh, with what we believe to be mo- a liquid mostly composed of, uh, uh, uh, quicksilver.

%% u3470:0-30, u3471:0-111, u3472:0-14 %%
Seeker: Uh, and it's very interesting. Actually, I recall now that I went on a school trip because it was a place to, to demonstrate density concepts. Uh, you could-

%% u3473:0-2 %%
Wellby: Mm

%% u3474:0-49, u3475:0-128, u3476:0-52 %%
Seeker: ... for example, float a solid bar of gold in it. You had to be careful not to drop it in, though, because it would sink and then only very slowly rise back again to the surface. They cautioned us not to swim in it for some reason.

%% u3477:0-72, u3478:0-15 %%
Wellby: When you went on a field trip, your teacher brought a solid bar of gold? That's amazing.

%% u3479:0-30 %%
Unknown: A wealthy school. Uh [laughs].

%% u3480:0-15 %%
Wellby: That's so cool.

%% u3481:0-5 %%
DM: Uh...

%% u3482:0-33 %%
Wellby: Are there things that live in it?

%% u3483:0-27, u3484:0-105 %%
Seeker: Not that we've encountered. The s- It was unpleasant to breathe in that chamber, as I recall, so you couldn't stay in there too long.

%% u3485:0-35 %%
Unknown: Is this far underground or above...

%% u3486:0-9 %%
Delwath: Far deep?

%% u3487:0-23 %%
Seeker: Quite, quite deep, yes.

%% u3488:0-18, u3489:0-25 %%

%% u3490:0-4 %%
Unknown: the-

%% u3491:0-61, u3492:0-28, u3493:0-74 %%

%% u3494:0-4 %%
Unknown: Hmm.

%% u3495:0-80, u3496:0-24, u3497:0-67 %%
Delwath: They were the, the scourge of the West in, um, during the Great War, these great beasts, fiendish beasts, 30, 40 feet tall with flaming whips burning the forest behind them.

%% u3498:0-48 %%
Seeker: We never saw anything like that on a field trip.

%% u3499:0-12, u3500:0-22, u3501:0-19 %%
Delwath: [laughs] Ah. I think they came from Cha'mutte. Uh, but-

%% u3502:0-62 %%
Wellby: Are there roads underground then that the dwarves didn't make?

%% u3503:0-21 %%
Delwath: That's what she said.

%% u3504:0-27 %%
Seeker: That's definitely the case.

%% u3505:0-25, u3506:0-32, u3507:0-39, u3508:0-19, u3509:0-36 %%
Delwath: She said they would go... She went deep beneath the Earth. She was many generations older than me. This must have been in the age of Drankor if not before.

%% u3510:0-3 %%
DM: Uh,

%% u3511:0-21, u3512:0-34, u3513:0-53, u3514:0-29, u3515:0-21, u3516:0-9 %%
Delwath: but there was a, a... She was part of a group that would go beneath the dwarven cities, uh, down for many days to the roots of the mountains and walk amongst them and hunt.

%% u3517:0-11, u3518:0-18 %%
Seeker: Wait, what? Hunt these fiends.

%% u3519:0-7, u3520:0-4 %%
Delwath: Fiends. Hmm.

%% u3521:0-64, u3522:0-33 %%

%% u3523:0-128, u3524:0-46, u3525:0-28, u3526:0-39, u3527:0-36, u3528:0-12, u3529:0-17 %%

I never personally have been down that far because obviously there's dangerous creatures down there, too. It's not all dwarvish transit.

%% u3530:0-4 %%
Unknown: Hmm.

%% u3531:0-50 %%
Seeker: What are these portals to the Plane of Stone like?

%% u3532:0-7 %%
Kenzo: I don't

%% u3533:0-5 %%
Unknown: know.

%% u3534:0-53 %%

%% u3535:0-73 %%
DM: I mean, it's like you can't really move through it unless you can burrow.

%% u3536:0-30, u3537:0-4 %%
Unknown: It's all solid and it's not... Hmm.

%% u3538:0-9 %%
Wellby: I wonder-

%% u3539:0-31, u3540:0-62 %%
DM: I mean, there's pockets of air. There's like pockets of gems and things, but there just seems-

%% u3541:0-25 %%
Unknown: There just seems between,

%% u3542:0-41, u3543:0-29 %%
Seeker: uh, materials and temperatures, gradients you can transit between them.

%% u3544:0-83, u3545:0-65 %%

%% u3546:0-74 %%
Delwath: Would you know when you left, uh, Taelgar and ended on the Plane of Stone?

%% u3547:0-6 %%
Wellby: Right.

%% u3548:0-26 %%
Delwath: Well, I wonder. Would you?

%% u3549:0-39, u3550:0-41 %%
Seeker: I would guess that I would know because someone less versed in geology might not.

%% u3551:0-89, u3552:0-7, u3553:0-127, u3554:0-32, u3555:0-47, u3556:0-54 %%
Seeker: There are strange phenomena down there at very high temperatures and pressures of course. Mm-hmm. But you would transition presumably to one where the world is made of a different material than you'd expect here on Taelgar or the way things moved or changed. What would stone be like given life and agency? Presumably it's all alive there in the Plane of Stone.

%% u3557:0-7 %%
Kenzo: Oh, no.

%% u3558:0-29 %%
Unknown: Kenzo's drifted off to sleep.

%% u3559:0-4 %%
Wellby: Hmm.

%% u3560:0-49 %%
Kenzo: Kenzo's, uh, bridging the land of the dreamworld.

%% u3561:0-21 %%
Seeker: Between worlds, yeah.

%% u3562:0-4 %%
Unknown: Hmm.

%% u3563:0-9 %%
Seeker: I'm here.

%% u3564:0-12 %%
Wellby: You're good.

%% u3565:0-52 %%
Unknown: Ah, it's a good time for Kenzo to take a little nap.

%% u3566:0-25, u3567:0-49 %%
Delwath: Yeah, it was the evening. Yeah. Kenzo's like e- eating too much fried fish.

%% u3568:0-50, u3569:0-28 %%
Unknown: Does, does food remind you of your, uh, childhood? Did you eat, eat fish a lot?

%% u3570:0-8, u3571:0-18, u3572:0-98, u3573:0-86 %%
Wellby: Yeah. We fished constantly. I, uh, you know, there were always lines going off the side of the boat when we were going across. It's the same sort of fish here, though they prepare it a lot more simply than we did.

%% u3574:0-9, u3575:0-41, u3576:0-54, u3577:0-36, u3578:0-11 %%

%% u3579:0-28 %%
Unknown: halfling food. It's so good.

%% u3580:0-26 %%
Wellby: Not like this human stuff.

%% u3581:0-4 %%
DM: Hmm.

%% u3582:0-30, u3583:0-10, u3584:0-17 %%

%% u3585:0-14 %%
DM: The elves are-

%% u3586:0-10, u3587:0-50 %%

%% u3588:0-58 %%
Delwath: Wellby was the one who said it was better than human food.

%% u3589:0-8 %%
Unknown: [laughs]

%% u3590:0-8, u3591:0-12 %%
DM: [laughs] I think, uh,

%% u3592:0-41 %%
Delwath: human food is a bit of an acquired taste.

%% u3593:0-97, u3594:0-141 %%

%% u3597:0-9 %%
Unknown: Oh, okay.

%% u3598:0-38 %%
Wellby: It tasted like it was 1,000 years old.

%% u3599:0-8 %%
Delwath: Oh, wow.

%% u3600:0-4 %%
Unknown: Hmm.

%% u3601:0-29 %%
Wellby: All right. I'll turn in, too.

%% u3602:0-3, u3603:0-43 %%
DM: So- Wait. Go ahead. You haven't said something.

DM: He's about to show up.

%% u3607:0-9 %%
Delwath: Oh, nice.

%% u3608:0-5, u3609:0-56, u3610:0-26, u3611:0-77, u3612:0-41, u3613:0-87 %%
DM: Cool. So as you are finishing talking about food, um, you see, you see walking up, um, a, a, a man also dressed heavily in furs as is, um- As is, you know, the sort of standard around here, um, dressed in, um, like furs but also like a thick sh- like sealskin jacket against the wind and the rain.

%% u3614:0-27, u3615:0-22, u3616:0-51, u3617:0-45, u3618:0-39 %%
DM: Um, not, he's not a priest. He's not tattooed. Uh, he, he sort of approaches a bit, a bit tentatively, kind of looking at all these, like, you know, he's probably never seen an elf before.

%% u3619:0-8 %%
Unknown: [laughs]

%% u3620:0-24, u3621:0-15, u3622:0-10, u3623:0-31, u3624:0-17 %%
DM: Um, Vaallinen said, "You wish to speak?" He sort of edges his way towards the fire. "May, may I sit?"

%% u3625:0-4 %%
Delwath: Yes.

%% u3626:0-7 %%
Seeker: Please.

%% u3627:0-7 %%
Delwath: Please.

%% u3628:0-7, u3629:0-13, u3630:0-15, u3631:0-26 %%
DM: "Can I, what can I... Can I help you? How can I, what can I do?"

%% u3632:0-19 %%
Delwath: Tell us your story.

%% u3633:0-35, u3634:0-34 %%
DM: He sort of shudders slightly a bit. "It's not, it's not a good story."

%% u3635:0-66 %%
Kenzo: We are heading there in the morning, and anything you can remember

%% u3636:0-36, u3637:0-25, u3638:0-31, u3639:0-39 %%
Delwath: may help us succeed at restoring it, even the smallest detail. I know it's hard to recall, but whatever you can would be very helpful.

%% u3640:0-14, u3641:0-10, u3642:0-27, u3643:0-8, u3644:0-31, u3645:0-10, u3646:0-2, u3647:0-39, u3648:0-47, u3649:0-14, u3650:0-26, u3651:0-3, u3652:0-5, u3653:0-16, u3654:0-119, u3655:0-3, u3656:0-13, u3657:0-31, u3658:0-12, u3659:0-71 %%
DM: "It was night. Weren't e- weren't expecting anything. I had... Don't, don't usually stay over. I often... I, I, I used to sail back, back and forth. I brought food, brought supplies, brought wood, took pilgrims. It was, it was the spring, and cold, and I decided to just sleep. I, I thought, 'Mm, I'll, I'll just spend the night in the longhouse, the pilgrims' longhouse at the docks.' And the island is safe. It's never been attacked. How could... I think now I should have gone, but how could, how could I have known?"

%% u3660:0-61, u3661:0-20, u3662:0-7, u3663:0-19, u3664:0-45, u3665:0-3, u3666:0-18, u3667:0-39, u3668:0-33, u3669:0-6, u3670:0-77, u3671:0-9, u3672:0-36, u3673:0-22 %%
DM: He looks around nervously. "The, the fire is what I remember. The ship docked, and a great explosion of flame, burning flame that cracked open in a flash of the pilgrims. I had... I think the only reason I survived was, uh, maybe it was Kaikkea's doing. I had, I had woken up to relieve myself in the night when I shone the, saw the ship, and I ran as the fires consumed the longhouse, cracking and burning."

%% u3674:0-37 %%
Seeker: You didn't see what started the fire?

%% u3675:0-46 %%
DM: "It came from one of the, the captain's hand."

%% u3676:0-4 %%
Unknown: Hmm.

%% u3677:0-6, u3678:0-7, u3679:0-28 %%
DM: Cast a flaming ball of fire from his hands.

%% u3680:0-11 %%
Delwath: Hmm, I see.

%% u3681:0-4 %%
Seeker: Hmm.

%% u3682:0-9, u3683:0-9 %%
Delwath: Fireball. Sorcerer.

%% u3684:0-16, u3685:0-68, u3686:0-13, u3687:0-85, u3688:0-43 %%
DM: And then his men stormed off the ship, killing everybody who ran out of the building. I was hiding. And once everybody was dead, I could hear the laughs and smell the burning flesh, and I don't really remember the next few hours.

%% u3689:0-21 %%
Wellby: How did you get away?

%% u3690:0-33, u3691:0-25, u3692:0-12, u3693:0-45, u3694:0-30, u3695:0-19, u3696:0-75 %%
DM: I don't really remember. I was... Sort of came to my senses in a rowboat a mile offshore. I must have ran in terror or did something once they had... I just remember the thud of their boots and the fires that flickered from their swords as they-

%% u3697:0-10 %%
Seeker: Did you...

%% u3698:0-83 %%
Delwath: The one who cast the, who ignited the initial fire, did you get a good look at him?

%% u3699:0-12, u3700:0-27 %%

%% u3701:0-13 %%
Delwath: Was he human?

%% u3702:0-12, u3703:0-11, u3704:0-18, u3705:0-62 %%
DM: He wore this helmet with demon horns on it, but from what I could see of his face, he seemed human enough.

%% u3706:0-25 %%
Unknown: Anyone with a skull face?

%% u3707:0-13 %%
DM: A skull face?

%% u3708:0-20 %%
Seeker: Something not human.

%% u3709:0-27 %%
Delwath: Creatures from a nightmare.

%% u3710:0-24, u3711:0-20, u3712:0-16, u3713:0-50, u3714:0-18 %%

%% u3715:0-16 %%
Seeker: Hmm. That it is.

%% u3716:0-33, u3717:0-4 %%

%% u3718:0-40 %%

%% u3719:0-10, u3720:0-15, u3721:0-37 %%

%% u3722:0-36 %%
Seeker: And they stay up there in the night?

%% u3723:0-65 %%
DM: The priests, th- there's a, they have some, some houses up there.

%% u3724:0-15 %%
Seeker: In the caldera?

%% u3725:0-15, u3726:0-44, u3727:0-54, u3728:0-15, u3729:0-39 %%
